gain : 0.0f;\n            default: return gain * (2.0f * t - 1.0f);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\nStim ramp(float lo, float hi, std::size_t period) {\n    if (g_seed) {  // hidden: same range, random rate and phase\n        std::uniform_real_distribution<float> u(0.0f, 1.0f);\n        auto p = static_cast<std::size_t>(static_cast<float>(period) * (0.05f + 3.0f * u(g_hidden_rng))) + 1;\n        auto off = static_cast<std::size_t>(u(g_hidden_rng) * static_cast<float>(p));\n        return [=](std::size_t n) { return lo + (hi - lo) * frac(n + off, p); };\n    }\n    return [=](std::size_t n) { return lo + (hi - lo) * frac(n, period); };\n}\nStim constant(float v) { return [=](std::size_t) { return v; }; }\n\nstruct Case {\n    const char* name;\n    Opcode op;\n    std::vector<Stim> inputs;      // wired to inputs[0..]; remaining slots NONE\n    std::uint16_t first_unused = 0; // inputs >= this index are NONE\n};\n","numLines":140,"startLine":1,"totalLines":342}}}

With a table, the\n    // main loop stops at the main/body boundary.\n    const std::size_t main_end = (slot->block_count == 0)\n                                     ? program.size()\n                                     : static_cast<std::size_t>(slot->main_count);\n    auto main_span = program.subspan(0, main_end);\n    std::size_t ip = 0;\n    while (ip < main_end) {\n        const Instruction& inst = main_span[ip];\n        if (inst.opcode == Opcode::POLY_BEGIN) {\n            ip = execute_poly_block(slot, program, ip);\n        } else if (inst.opcode == Opcode::FOREACH_EVENT) {\n            // L3 dispatch-loop opcode: run the subprogram body from the\n            // table. The body is not inline — advance by exactly one.\n            execute_foreach_event(slot, program, ip);\n            ++ip;\n        } else if (inst.opcode == Opcode::EVENT_MAP) {\n            // prd-runtime-event-transforms: closure form needs slot->blocks[],\n            // so it dispatches here; the Phase-1 packed form has no body.\n            if (inst.flags & InstructionFlag::EVENT_CLOSURE) {\n                run_event_map_closure(slot, inst);\n            } else {\n                op_event_map(ctx_, inst);\n            }\n            ++ip;\n        } else if (inst.opcode == Opcode::EVENT_FILTER) {\n            if (inst.flags & InstructionFlag::EVENT_CLOSURE) {\n                run_event_filter_closure(slot, inst);\n            } else {\n                op_event_filter(ctx_, inst);\n            }\n            ++ip;\n        } else {\n            ip = execute_step(slot, main_span, ip);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nstd::size_t VM::execute_poly_block(const ProgramSlot* slot,\n                                   std::span<const Instruction> program,\n                                   std::size_t ip) {\n    const auto& poly_inst = program[ip];\n    const std::uint8_t body_length = poly_inst.rate;\n\n    // Legacy POLY_BEGIN: body is inline at program[ip+1 .. ip+body_length].\n    auto& poly_state = state_pool_.get_or_create<PolyAllocState>(poly_inst.state_id);\n    run_voice_pool(slot,\n                   poly_state,\n                   poly_inst.out_buffer,\n                   poly_inst.inputs[0], poly_inst.inputs[4],\n                   program.subspan(ip + 1, body_length));\n\n    // Advance past POLY_BEGIN + body + POLY_END\n    return ip + 1 + body_length + 1;\n}\n\n// FOREACH_EVENT — table-based subprogram dispatch (PRD L3). The body lives in\n// the ProgramSlot subprogram table, not inline; the dispatch loop advances by\n// exactly one after this returns.\nvoid VM::execute_foreach_event(const ProgramSlot* slot,\n                               std::span<const Instruction> program,\n                               std::size_t ip) {\n    const auto& inst = program[ip];\n\n    // Resolve the per-instance state. init_foreach_state created exactly one\n    // of these three types for this state_id, so the first non-null wins and\n    // selects the allocator kind.\n    //\n    // Each successful branch must mark the state as touched. begin_frame()\n    // clears the touched-flag array at the top of every execute_program\n    // call, and gc_sweep() (run at crossfade completion) evicts every\n    // untouched slot. get_or_create / get touch implicitly; get_if does\n    // not — so without an explicit touch here, a structural hot-swap that\n    // crossfades evicts PolyAllocState / Foreach{Iter,Shared}State even\n    // though the new program is still using it, silently breaking\n    // event-driven voice synthesis.\n    if (auto* poly_state = state_pool_.get_if<PolyAllocState>(inst.state_id)) {\n        state_pool_.touch(inst.state_id);\n        // VOICE_POOL — bit-exact with legacy POLY: identical run_voice_pool,\n        // identical convention slots; only the body location differs.\n        const auto body = slot->block_body(poly_state->block_id);\n        run_voice_pool(slot,\n                       *poly_state,\n                       inst.out_buffer,\n                       inst.inputs[0], inst.inputs[4],\n                       body);\n        return;\n    }\n    if (auto* iter_state = state_pool_.get_if<ForeachIterState>(inst.state_id)) {\n        state_pool_.touch(inst.state_id);\n        const auto body = slot->block_body(iter_state->block_id);\n        run_foreach_per_iteration(slot, *iter_state, inst, body);\n        return;\n    }\n    if (auto* shared_state = state_pool_.get_if<ForeachSharedState>(inst.state_id)) {\n        state_pool_.touch(inst.state_id);\n        const auto body = slot->block_body(shared_state->block_id);\n        run_foreach_shared(slot, *shared_state, inst, body);\n        return;\n    }\n    // No state — uninitialized FOREACH_EVENT (init_foreach_state did not run).\n    // Skip silently; the dispatch loop still advances by one.\n}\n\n// BLOCK_CALL — table-based shared-`fn` dispatch (PRD L2). The tree is back to the accepted state; the last check reported \"empty patch\", which confirms no files changed. I used 4 of the 10 check runs.\n\n**What bounds these opcodes:** in the benchmark, each opcode instance writes to its own output buffer. The 64 outputs together are 32 KB, but they sit 1 KB apart, so they only use half of L1's sets and keep evicting each other. Every block, all 8 output cache lines are fetched from L2 and 8 modified lines are written back. That is about 1 KB of L2 traffic in roughly 10 ns, which is near what one core's L2 can move. The loads, arithmetic and call overhead are small next to that. Earlier attempts already tried prefetch timing, store order, unrolling, inline asm and cache hints.\n\n**What I tried:** an extra kernel tier for CPUs with AVX-512F, detected once at start-up in `simd.cpp`. It writes each output cache line with one 64-byte store instead of two 32-byte stores, which halves the number of stores that stall waiting for a line from L2. The results are exact IEEE add/sub/mul, so output stays bit-identical, and it would switch off along with AVX2 when the ISA is forced lower.\n\n- **First check (aligned 64-byte stores):** build and bit-identity passed; speed was add 1.00x, sub 1.01x, mul 1.01x.\n- **Second check (alignment requirement removed, unaligned stores):** add 1.02x, sub 1.03x, mul 1.00x. That's still within noise.\n\nUnaligned 512-bit stores should have changed the timing if that code ran, so the gate CPU most likely has no AVX-512F. Since it gave nothing here and would add untested code, I reverted it rather than leave it in for other machines.\n\nI also skipped two ideas that would likely have sped up this benchmark but made real patches slower:\n- **Non-temporal stores for some outputs:** those outputs would skip the cache, so the next opcode reading them would go to main memory.\n- **Pushing output lines out of L1 right after writing (`cldemote`):** the next opcode usually reads that output straight away and would miss L1.\n\nWith this buffer layout, which I'm not allowed to change, I don't see a further legitimate kernel-side gain for these three opcodes.\n\nIDEA: output-block stores bound by L2 fill/write-back traffic (1 KB-strided outputs thrash half the L1 sets) -> added a start-up-detected AVX-512F tier with one 64-byte store per output line for add/sub/mul, then reverted it (check showed 1.00–1.03x, within noise; the gate CPU apparently lacks AVX-512F, so the tree is left at the accepted state)"}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":341,"cache_read_input_tokens":50284,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":341},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f525cb40-7f4a-4493-bbd5-35dedb07816d","uuid":"af706446-1db1-436d-8f9c-18dcb3a8a2ca","timestamp":"2026-09-25T17:39:34.251Z","request_id":"req_011CfQZp9yk2AXYPTZ1CzXTA"}
